	/*Global Modules*/







	function getQueryString() {







		var querystring = new Array;







		var q = String(document.location).split("?")[1];







		if (!q) {







			return false







		}







		q = q.split("&");







		for (var i = 0; i < q.length; i++) {







			var o = q[i].split("=");







			querystring[o[0]] = o[1]







		}







		return querystring







	}















	function getHashString() {







		var qs = new Array;







		var q = String(document.location).split("#")[1];







		if (!q) {







			return false







		}







		q = q.split("&");







		for (var i = 0; i < q.length; i++) {







			var o = q[i].split("=");







			qs[o[0]] = o[1]







		}







		return qs







	}















</script>

<script>
    (function() {
        var $hpVideo = jQuery('.hpVideoAuto');
        var $hpVideoLinks = $hpVideo.siblings('a');
        var imgpath = "/web_assets/img/20191025/hp_hero_poster.jpg";
        var img = new Image();
        img.src = imgpath;
        $hpVideo.on('ended', function() {
            $hpVideo.attr("poster", "/web_assets/img/20191025/hp_hero_poster.jpg");
            $hpVideo.attr('controls', false);
            $hpVideo.autoplay = false;
            $('video')[0].load();
            $('video')[0].pause();
            $hpVideoLinks.filter('#playVideo').show();
            $hpVideoLinks.filter('#videoLink').hide();
        });
        jQuery('#playVideo').on('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            $hpVideoLinks.filter('#playVideo').hide();
            $hpVideo.attr('controls', true);
            $hpVideo.get(0).play();
            $hpVideo[0].muted = false;
        });
    })();

</script>
<script>
    /*************************************************************************
     ** Video Lazy Load (can move to global config for all brands if needed)
     ***************************************************************************/
    (function() {
        var videoLazyLoadDetect = function() {
            try {
                var $videoLazyLoad = $('video.lazyload-video');
                if ($videoLazyLoad.length) {
                    $videoLazyLoad.each(function() {
                        var $vid = $(this);
                        if ($vid.is(':visible')) {
                            var $videoSourceElem = $vid.find('source');
                            var videoMainSrc = $vid.filter('data-src').length || '';
                            var src = (videoMainSrc) ? $vid.attr('data-src') : $videoSourceElem.attr(
                                'data-src');

                            if (src) {
                                var $vidContent = $vid[0];
                                $vidContent.pause();
                                $vid.removeClass('lazyload').addClass('lazyloaded-video');
                                (videoMainSrc) ? $vid.attr('src', src): $videoSourceElem.attr('src',
                                    src);
                                $vidContent.load();
                                $vidContent.play();;
                            }
                        }
                    })
                } else {
                    jQuery(window).off('resize scroll', videoLazyLoadDetect);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.log('LAZYLOAD ERROR: ' + err);
            }
        }
        jQuery(window).on('resize scroll', videoLazyLoadDetect).resize();
    })();

</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    var initExtole = function() {
        $(".promo-offers-nav .extole-dwp").attr("id", "extole-dwp-xs");
        $("#carousel-footer-lg .extole-dwp").attr("id", "extole-dwp-lg");
        /* Start Extole */
        (function(c, e, k, l, a) {
            c[e] = c[e] || {};
            for (c[e].q = c[e].q || []; a < l.length;) k(l[a++], c[e])
        })(window, "extole", function(c, e) {
            e[c] = e[c] || function() {
                e.q.push([c, arguments])
            }
        }, ["createZone"], 0);
        /* End Extole */
        extole.createZone({
            name: 'promo_drawer_desktop',
            element_id: 'extole-dwp-lg'
        });
        extole.createZone({
            name: 'promo_drawer_mobile',
            element_id: 'extole-dwp-xs'
        });
    };
    //IF THE DRAWER PROMO ISN"T MOVED TO THE NAV THEN INIT EXTOLE
    if ($('#promo-footer[data-promo-drawer-display="alt"]').length === -1) {
        setTimeout(function() {
            initExtole();
        }, 500);
    } else {
        jQuery(window).on('drawerPromoMoved', function() {
            setTimeout(function() {
                initExtole();
            }, 500);
        });
    }

</script>
